> 65	### Arquitetura Swarm Arena
> 66	
> 67	```
> 68	openrouter-swarm (Edge Function):
> 69	  POST { prompt, model_ids[] }  ← JWT obrigatório (Zero-Trust)
> 70	  → Promise.all([callModel(m1), callModel(m2), callModel(m3)])
> 71	      callModel:
> 72	        AbortController (30s timeout)
> 73	        fetch https://openrouter.ai/api/v1/chat/completions
> 74	        mede latency_ms via performance.now()
> 75	        retorna ModelResult (output | error) — nunca throw
> 76	  → rank: successful ordenados por latency_ms, errors ao final
> 77	  → SwarmResponse { results[], fastest_model, total_duration_ms, timestamp }
> 78	
> 79	useSwarmArena (hook):
> 80	  models: ModelConfig[]  ← toggleModel(id) → enabled/disabled
> 81	  runMutation:
> 82	    1. supabase.functions.invoke('openrouter-swarm', { prompt, model_ids })
> 83	    2. INSERT swarm_arena_runs (user_id, prompt, model_ids, results, fastest_model, total_duration_ms)
> 84	    3. qc.invalidateQueries → historyQuery reloads
> 85	  historyQuery: TanStack Query → swarm_arena_runs ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 20
> 86	  chartData: ModelResult[] → [{ name, latency, color, error, rank }] para Recharts BarChart
> 87	
> 88	ModelArenaPage (/dashboard/arena):
> 89	  ├── Header Enterprise (Cpu icon + title + last run timestamp)
> 90	  ├── Winner Banner (Trophy + fastest_model + latency)
> 91	  ├── LatencyChart (Recharts BarChart — Cell colorido por modelo, erro = vermelho)
> 92	  ├── Model Grid (3 ModelCards — Switch, latency badge, output preview, rank)
> 93	  ├── Controls: Stress Test (prompt fixo) + Custom Prompt (Textarea)
> 94	  └── History Panel (TanStack Query — últimas 20 corridas Supabase)
> 95	
> 96	swarm_arena_runs (migration 20260426180000):
> 97	  id uuid PK · user_id (RLS) · prompt text · model_ids text[]
> 98	  results jsonb · fastest_model text · total_duration_ms int · created_at timestamptz
> 99	  INDEX: (user_id, created_at DESC)
> 100	
> 101	LinkedIn resilience (useSocialAccounts.ts):
> 102	  initiateOAuth captura:
> 103	    error.message → toast se contém "LinkedIn not configured" / "Instagram" / "Twitter"
> 104	    data.error (HTTP 500 body) → toast específico por plataforma
> 105	  Secrets necessários (ainda pendentes de configuração):
> 106	    npx supabase secrets set LINKEDIN_CLIENT_ID=<id>
> 107	    npx supabase secrets set LINKEDIN_CLIENT_SECRET=<secret>
> 108	```

> 129	### Arquitetura de Bloqueadores (Affiliate Engine)
> 130	
> 131	```
> 132	BLOQUEADOR 1 — process-affiliate-link (supabase/functions/process-affiliate-link/index.ts)
> 133	  Estado atual: skeleton, retorna { affiliate_url: null }
> 134	  Impacto: nenhum link afiliado ML é gerado → zero comissão
> 135	  Fix:
> 136	    POST https://api.mercadolibre.com/affiliate_links (ML Affiliates API)
> 137	    → UTM wrap: ?utm_source=mercadolivre&utm_medium=social
> 138	                 &utm_campaign={campaign_id}&utm_term={keyword}
> 139	    → INSERT affiliate_links (short_url, original_url, campaign_id)
> 140	    → disparar embed-mcorch-node para o link node

> 143	  Estado atual: criado com 0, nunca atualizado
> 144	  Impacto: ROI Dashboard mostra R$0 para sempre
> 145	  Fix:
> 146	    Nova edge function: handle-ml-postback
> 147	    → Recebe webhook de conversão do ML

> 149	    → UPDATE mcorch_nodes SET revenue_impact = X (knowledge mesh sync)

> 152	  Estado atual: "magic_constellation_v1" hardcoded (orchestrate-content/index.ts:90)
> 153	  Impacto: 100 campanhas → 1 linha no painel ML, sem atribuição individual
> 154	  Fix: passar campaign_id como parâmetro → utm_campaign={campaign_id}

> 185	### Arquitetura Phase Epsilon V8 + Delta V4
> 186	
> 187	```
> 188	Delta v4 — Two-Pass Ingestion (parser.ts):
> 189	  Pass 1 (existing):
> 190	    visitFile(file) → nodes + intra-file CALLS edges → mergeIntoGraph
> 191	  Post-pass:
> 192	    globalFunctionMap = Map<name, nodeId>  (all function/arrow_function nodes)
> 193	  Pass 2 (new):
> 194	    for each file:
> 195	      localFunctionIds = file's own function nodes (from graph.fileIndex)
> 196	      visitFileForCrossFileCalls(file, local, global)
> 197	        → extractCrossFileCalls: re-parses AST, walks CallExpression
> 198	        → emits CALLS edge only if callee NOT in local map BUT IN global map
> 199	        → dedup via edgeId hash (graph.edges.set overwrites by ID)
> 200	  Result: +259 cross-file CALLS · 646 total edges in mesh
> 201	
> 202	Epsilon v8 — CALLS counter (useConstellationSigma → ConstellationMatrix):
> 203	  Pre-Phase 1:
> 204	    callsOutMap: Map<nodeId, count> — from astEdges where relation_type = 'calls'
> 205	    callsInMap:  Map<nodeId, count> — incoming CALLS per node
> 206	  Phase 1 (addNode):
> 207	    g.addNode(id, { ..., callsOut: callsOutMap.get(id) ?? 0, callsIn: callsInMap.get(id) ?? 0 })
> 208	  enterNode → setTooltip({ ..., callsOut: attrs.callsOut, callsIn: attrs.callsIn })
> 209	  Tooltip render (function/arrow_function only):
> 210	    [→ N] cyan   — outgoing calls (funções que este nó invoca)
> 211	    [← M] fuchsia — incoming calls (funções que invocam este nó)
> 212	
> 213	DB state post Delta v4 sync (2026-04-26):
> 214	  mcorch_nodes: 526 (unchanged)
> 215	  mcorch_edges: 646 (454 defines/calls-intra + 192 cross-file calls)
> 216	  Unresolved:  2002 (defines to variable/import/class/enum — not in SYNCABLE_KINDS)
> 217	```

> 268	        [pill content] [pill tech] [pill market] [pill ops]
> 269	        Each pill: border + bg tinted in squad color
> 270	
> 271	Delta v3b DB state (post sync-mesh 2026-04-26):
> 272	  mcorch_nodes: 526 (493 original + 33 new)
> 273	  mcorch_edges: 454 (344 defines + 110 calls resolved)
> 274	  Unresolved:  1999 (calls to nodes not in sync — file nodes + cross-file)
> 275	```
> 276	
> 277	### Delta v3b — Por que 1999 edges ficaram unresolved
> 278	
> 279	O sync-mesh só persiste `function / arrow_function / interface` nodes. Edges `CALLS` que apontam para:
> 280	1. **Arrow functions declaradas como variáveis** (`const fn = () => ...`) — o endpoint existe no grafo AST como `variable`, mas não é sincronizado
> 281	2. **Funções em outros arquivos** — o `functionNameMap` é por-arquivo; imports não são resolvidos cross-file
> 282	3. **Built-ins / externos** — chamadas a `console.log`, `Array.map`, etc.
> 283	
> 284	Solução para Delta v4: incluir `variable` nodes com `node_type = 'arrow_function'` no sync-mesh.
> 285

> 385	### Arquitetura do Crew Overlay
> 386	
> 387	```
> 388	useCrewOverlay (TanStack Query)
> 389	  ├── crew_agents WHERE user_id = auth.uid()
> 390	  ├── inferSquad(name, role) → content | tech | market | ops | default
> 391	  └── SQUAD_PATTERNS[squad] → file name patterns para matching
> 392	
> 393	useConstellationSigma({ overlayAgents })
> 394	  └── Phase 6 (após community coloring):
> 395	        ├── Para cada agent: match file nodes por pattern (case-insensitive)
> 396	        ├── Centróide dos matches → posição de órbita (±120u)
> 397	        ├── addNode crew_agent__{id} — size=20, color=squad color, isAgent=true
> 398	        └── addEdge INHABITS → até 4 file nodes matched
> 399	
> 400	ConstellationMatrix
> 401	  ├── pulsePhaseRef (0…2π) — incremento 0.12 a cada 80ms
> 402	  ├── nodeReducer: kind=crew_agent → size*(1+sin(phase)*0.35), forceLabel, zIndex=10
> 403	  ├── edgeReducer: kind=INHABITS → cor do squad + opacity 0.35
> 404	  └── darkSpotCount = file nodes sem nenhum agente cobrindo
> 405	```

> 583	### Estado dos edges (diagnóstico)
> 584	
> 585	O extrator AST atual emite apenas edges estruturais (`file → function/interface` = `defines`). Edges de call-graph function→function requerem análise de call sites no TypeScript visitor — enhancement para Delta v3.
> 586	
> 587	```
> 588	Edges totais no grafo:  2145
> 589	  → defines (file→fn/interface): 344  ← sincronizados ✅
> 590	  → import/variable endpoints:  1801  ← não sincronizados (fora do scope)
> 591	```
> 592	
> 593	### Sync idempotente — como executar
> 594	```bash
> 595	cd packages/gitnexus-core
> 596	npm run sync-mesh   # lê .env da raiz do projeto automaticamente
> 597	# Segunda execução → 0 inserções (dedup por ast_node_id)
> 598	```

> 683	## Error #185 Fix Record (2026-04-26)
> 684	
> 685	Root cause: `useSimulation` subscribed to entire Zustand store + `agents`/`edges` in `useEffect` deps. Every `updateAgentStatus` call created new array refs → effect cleanup + new interval → cascade under React 18 concurrent mode → Maximum Update Depth Exceeded + WebGL context loss.
> 686	
> 687	| File | Bug | Fix |
> 688	|------|-----|-----|
> 689	| `useSimulation.ts` | `agents`/`edges` in `useEffect` deps — recreated interval on every store update | `getState()` inside interval — zero deps, single interval, always-fresh reads |
> 690	| `SquadHull.tsx` | `filter()` inline selector — new array ref on every store update, SquadHull re-rendered every 3s regardless of squad | `useShallow` — element-by-element comparison, only re-renders when squad agents change |
> 691	| `CrewGraph.tsx` | `SQUAD_META` inside `Scene` render body — recreated every render | Hoisted to module scope |
> 692	| `AgentEdge.tsx` | Glow `<points>` sharing mutable `Float32Array` without `needsUpdate` — GPU waste + potential context pressure | Removed broken element |
> 693	
> 694	| Commit | Conteúdo |
> 695	|--------|----------|
> 696	| `b1e1d1f` | Error #185 root cause + 3 secondary stability fixes |
> 697

> 703	
> 704	| Bug | Root Cause | Fix |
> 705	|-----|-----------|-----|
> 706	| Infinite spinner | `setLoaded(true)` nunca chamado em query error | `edgesQuery.isSuccess / isError` flags em `useCrewData.ts` |
> 707	| Font 404 crash | `font="/fonts/Inter-Bold.woff"` → arquivo inexistente | Removido prop `font` de `SquadHull.tsx` (drei usa Roboto CDN) |
> 708	| THREE.js buffer resize crash | `particleCount` 24↔10 redimensionava BufferAttribute in-place | `key={particleCount}` em `<points>` força remount completo em `AgentEdge.tsx` |
> 709	| WebGL Context Lost (crash loop 60fps) | `@react-three/postprocessing@3.0.4` lê `__r3f.children` (API R3F v9) mas projeto usa R3F v8.18 que expõe `__r3f.objects` → `undefined.length` | Downgrade para `@react-three/postprocessing@2.19.1` + `PostProcessing = memo(...)` em `CrewGraph.tsx` |

> 747	## Affiliate Engine Ignitron Record (2026-04-26)
> 748	
> 749	| Action | Result |
> 750	|--------|--------|
> 751	| `supabase/functions/process-affiliate-link/index.ts` — lógica real | ✅ `probeUrl` (HEAD 8s, 403=existe) · `extractKeywords` (URL slug parse) · `findSimilarProduct` (ML search API) · `buildAffiliateUrl` (partner_id + UTMs) |
> 752	| Fluxo de self-heal | ✅ URL quebrada → extrai keywords do path → ML search API → produto similar como fallback → 422 se não houver substituto |
> 753	| Resolução de affiliate_id | ✅ `affiliate_config.app_id` do usuário → fallback `ML_AFFILIATE_ID` secret → `null` (sem partner_id, só UTMs) |
> 754	| Persistência em `affiliate_links` | ✅ `original_url` · `short_url` (URL monetizada) · `campaign_id` · `content_id` · `metadata.self_healed_from` |
> 755	| `ContentLibraryPage.tsx` — detector ML URLs | ✅ Regex `/https?:\/\/(?:[\w-]+\.)?mercadolivre\.com\.br\/[^\s"'),<>]+/g` sobre `item.body` |
> 756	| `ContentLibraryPage.tsx` — dropdown "Monetizar Links ML" | ✅ Aparece em itens de texto com URLs ML detectadas · `DollarSign` icon emerald · loading state `Loader2` |
> 757	| `ContentLibraryPage.tsx` — badge 💰 ML | ✅ Badge emerald `border-emerald-500/40` exibido quando tag `afiliado-ml` presente · adicionada automaticamente após monetização |
> 758	| `ContentLibraryPage.tsx` — `handleMonetizeLinks` | ✅ Deduplica URLs · chama edge function por URL · substitui todas as ocorrências no body · upsert tag `afiliado-ml` |
> 759	| Build de produção | ✅ Zero erros, 21.03s — ContentLibraryPage: 38.26 kB gzip |
> 760	| Deploy `process-affiliate-link` | ✅ 76.11 kB · live em `bcyvddsykvehvpwstlfa` |
> 761	
> 762	### Arquitetura Affiliate Engine Ignitron
> 763	
> 764	```
> 765	process-affiliate-link (Edge Function):
> 766	  POST { product_url, campaign_id?, content_id? }  ← JWT obrigatório
> 767	
> 768	  1. Resolve affiliate_id:
> 769	       affiliate_config (user DB) → ML_AFFILIATE_ID (env secret) → null
> 770	
> 771	  2. probeUrl(product_url):
> 772	       HEAD request 8s · OK/403/405 = exists
> 773	       → if false: self-heal path
> 774	
> 775	  3. Self-heal (se URL quebrada):
> 776	       extractKeywords(url) → slug parse → keywords string
> 777	       findSimilarProduct(kw) → GET ML search API ?q= limit=1
> 778	       → resolve = fallback permalink
> 779	       → 422 se ML API também falhar (geo-block não afeta Supabase Cloudflare)
> 780	
> 781	  4. buildAffiliateUrl(resolved, affiliateId, campaign):
> 782	       new URL(resolved)
> 783	       .set("partner_id", affiliateId)  ← se disponível
> 784	       .set("utm_source", "mcorch")
> 785	       .set("utm_medium", "social")
> 786	       .set("utm_campaign", campaign)
> 787	
> 788	  5. INSERT affiliate_links:
> 789	       original_url · short_url · platform=mercadolivre
> 790	       campaign_id · content_id · metadata{affiliate_id, resolved_url, self_healed_from}
> 791	
> 792	  6. Return: {success, original_url, resolved_url, affiliate_url, self_healed, link_id}
> 793	
> 794	ContentLibraryPage monetization flow:
> 795	  Card (text, body contains mercadolivre.com.br)
> 796	    └── DropdownMenu → "Monetizar Links ML"
> 797	          → handleMonetizeLinks(item):
> 798	               match(ML_URL_RE) → unique URLs
> 799	               for url of unique:
> 800	                 POST process-affiliate-link → affiliate_url
> 801	                 body.split(url).join(affiliate_url)
> 802	               updateContent({ body: updatedBody, tags: [..., "afiliado-ml"] })
> 803	          → Badge 💰 ML (emerald) exibido após monetização
> 804	```

> 960	## Video Rescue Protocol
> 961	
> 962	When a video generation operation (Google Veo) times out or client disconnects:
> 963	```bash
> 964	# Via edge function (production):
> 965	curl -X POST "https://bcyvddsykvehvpwstlfa.supabase.co/functions/v1/rescue-video" \
> 966	  -H "Authorization: Bearer <user_jwt>" \
> 967	  -d '{"content_id":"<uuid>","operation_id":"<operation_name>"}'
> 968	
> 969	# Via local script (admin/debug):
> 970	source .env && bun run rescue.ts <operation_id> <content_id>
> 971	```

> 975	## Architecture Pillars
> 976	
> 977	| Layer | Implementation |
> 978	|-------|---------------|
> 979	| **Infra** | Docker Sovereign Mesh (`mcorch_chroma` + `mcorch_claude_mem` + `mega-brain-daemon`) + `watchdog-mcorch.sh` telemetry |

> 182	                    <NavLink
> 183	                      to={item.url}
> 184	                      className="hover:bg-muted/50"
> 185	                      activeClassName="bg-primary/10 text-primary font-medium"
> 186	                    >

> 188	                      {!collapsed && <span>{item.title}</span>}
> 189	                    </NavLink>
> 190	                  </SidebarMenuButton>
> 191	                </SidebarMenuItem>
> 192	              ))}
> 193	            </SidebarMenu>
> 194	          </SidebarGroupContent>
> 195	        </SidebarGroup>
> 196	
> 197	        {isAdmin && (
> 198	          <SidebarGroup>
> 199	            <SidebarGroupLabel>Admin</SidebarGroupLabel>
> 200	            <SidebarGroupContent>
> 201	              <SidebarMenu>
> 202	                <SidebarMenuItem>
> 203	                  <SidebarMenuButton asChild>
> 204	                    <NavLink to="/dashboard/admin" className="hover:bg-muted/50" activeClassName="bg-primary/10 text-primary font-medium">
> 205	                      <Shield className="mr-2 h-4 w-4 shrink-0" />
> 206	                      {!collapsed && <span>Administração</span>}
> 207	                    </NavLink>
> 208	                  </SidebarMenuButton>
> 209	                </SidebarMenuItem>
> 210	              </SidebarMenu>
> 211	            </SidebarGroupContent>
> 212	          </SidebarGroup>
> 213	        )}
> 214	      </SidebarContent>
> 215	
> 216	      <SidebarFooter>
> 217	        <Button
> 218	          variant="ghost"
> 219	          size={collapsed ? 'icon' : 'default'}
> 220	          className="w-full justify-start text-muted-foreground hover:text-destructive"
> 221	          onClick={handleSignOut}
> 222	        >
> 223	          <LogOut className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0" />
> 224	          {!collapsed && <span className="ml-2">Sair</span>}
> 225	        </Button>
> 226	      </SidebarFooter>
> 227	    </Sidebar>
> 228	  );
> 229	}

```json
{
  "file_path": "/home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/.claude/worktrees/pensive-khorana-9b27f2/supabase/functions/handle-ml-postback/index.ts",
  "content": "import { createClient } from \"https://esm.sh/@supabase/supabase-js@2\";\n\nconst corsHeaders = {\n  \"Access-Control-Allow-Origin\": \"*\",\n  \"Access-Control-Allow-Headers\": \"authorization, x-api-key, content-type, x-ml-signature\",\n};\n\n// ML Affiliate postback payload \u2014 query params or JSON body\ninterface MLPostbackPayload {\n  click_id?: string;        // our affiliate_links.id (short tracking token)\n  order_id?: string;        // ML order reference\n  commission?: string;      // commission value (BRL cents or float)\n  currency?: string;        // BRL\n  status?: string;          // approved | pending | cancelled\n  transaction_id?: string;  // alternate key for click_id\n  // ML Notification API (topic=orders) shape\n  topic?: string;\n  resource?: string;\n  user_id?: string;\n}\n\nDeno.serve(async (req: Request) => {\n  if (req.method === \"OPTIONS\") return new Response(\"ok\", { headers: corsHeaders });\n\n  const supabase = createClient(\n    Deno.env.get(\"SUPABASE_URL\") ?? \"\",\n    Deno.env.get(\"SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY\") ?? \"\",\n  );\n\n  try {\n    // Parse payload \u2014 accept both query string (GET) and JSON body (POST)\n    let payload: MLPostbackPayload = {};\n    const url = new URL(req.url);\n\n    if (req.method === \"GET\" || req.method === \"HEAD\") {\n      url.searchParams.forEach((v, k) => { (payload as Record<string, string>)[k] = v; });\n    } else {\n      const ct = req.headers.get(\"content-type\") ?? \"\";\n      if (ct.includes(\"application/json\")) {\n        payload = await req.json();\n      } else if (ct.includes(\"application/x-www-form-urlencoded\")) {\n        const text = await req.text();\n        new URLSearchParams(text).forEach((v, k) => { (payload as Record<string, string>)[k] = v; });\n      } else {\n        // Try JSON anyway\n        try { payload = await req.json(); } catch { /* ignore */ }\n      }\n      // Also merge any query params (ML sometimes sends both)\n      url.searchParams.forEach((v, k) => { if (!payload[k as keyof MLPostbackPayload]) (payload as Record<string, string>)[k] = v; });\n    }\n\n    const clickId = payload.click_id ?? payload.transaction_id;\n    const status = (payload.status ?? \"approved\").toLowerCase();\n\n    // Only process approved conversions\n    if (status === \"cancelled\" || status === \"refunded\") {\n      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, action: \"skipped\", reason: `status=${status}` }), {\n        headers: { ...corsHeaders, \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\" },\n      });\n    }\n\n    // Parse commission \u2014 ML sends float (e.g. \"12.50\") or integer cents\n    let revenueCents = 0;\n    if (payload.commission) {\n      const raw = parseFloat(payload.commission);\n      // If value looks like cents already (>= 100 for amounts over R$1), keep as-is;\n      // if it looks like a float BRL (e.g. 12.50), convert to cents\n      revenueCents = raw < 100 ? Math.round(raw * 100) : Math.round(raw);\n    }\n\n    // Resolve the affiliate link record\n    // click_id can be either the uuid or the short_url path segment\n    let affiliateLinkId: string | null = null;\n\n    if (clickId) {\n      // Try UUID match first\n      const uuidMatch = clickId.match(/^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i);\n      if (uuidMatch) {\n        affiliateLinkId = clickId;\n      } else {\n        // Try matching against short_url or metadata.click_id\n        const { data } = await supabase\n          .from(\"affiliate_links\")\n          .select(\"id\")\n          .or(`short_url.ilike.%${clickId}%,metadata->click_id.eq.${clickId}`)\n          .limit(1)\n          .single();\n        affiliateLinkId = data?.id ?? null;\n      }\n    }\n\n    // Also resolve by order_id if available\n    if (!affiliateLinkId && payload.order_id) {\n      const { data } = await supabase\n        .from(\"affiliate_links\")\n        .select(\"id\")\n        .eq(\"metadata->>order_id\", payload.order_id)\n        .limit(1)\n        .single();\n      affiliateLinkId = data?.id ?? null;\n    }\n\n    if (!affiliateLinkId) {\n      console.warn(\"handle-ml-postback: could not resolve affiliate link\", payload);\n      // Return 200 to prevent ML from retrying (we log it)\n      await supabase.from(\"affiliate_links\").insert({\n        original_url: payload.resource ?? \"unknown\",\n        short_url: `unresolved:${payload.order_id ?? clickId ?? \"no-id\"}`,\n        platform: \"mercadolivre\",\n        revenue_cents: revenueCents,\n        metadata: { ...payload, _unresolved: true },\n      });\n      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, action: \"unresolved_logged\" }), {\n        headers: { ...corsHeaders, \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\" },\n      });\n    }\n\n    // Update revenue on the affiliate link\n    const { data: linkData, error: updateErr } = await supabase\n      .from(\"affiliate_links\")\n      .update({\n        revenue_cents: revenueCents,\n        metadata: {\n          order_id: payload.order_id,\n          currency: payload.currency ?? \"BRL\",\n          status,\n          postback_received_at: new Date().toISOString(),\n        },\n      })\n      .eq(\"id\", affiliateLinkId)\n      .select(\"id, content_id, campaign_id, metadata\")\n      .single();\n\n    if (updateErr) throw updateErr;\n\n    // Sync revenue_impact to mcorch_nodes (knowledge mesh)\n    // Find the node linked to this affiliate link (by metadata.link_id or content_id)\n    const { data: node } = await supabase\n      .from(\"mcorch_nodes\")\n      .select(\"id, revenue_impact\")\n      .or(`metadata->>link_id.eq.${affiliateLinkId},metadata->>content_id.eq.${linkData?.content_id ?? \"null\"}`)\n      .limit(1)\n      .single();\n\n    if (node) {\n      const newRevenue = (node.revenue_impact ?? 0) + revenueCents;\n      await supabase\n        .from(\"mcorch_nodes\")\n        .update({ revenue_impact: newRevenue })\n        .eq(\"id\", node.id);\n    }\n\n    // Insert a knowledge mesh observation node for this conversion event\n    await supabase.from(\"mcorch_nodes\").insert({\n      name: `conversion:${affiliateLinkId.slice(0, 8)}`,\n      node_type: \"observation\",\n      content: `ML conversion \u2014 R$${(revenueCents / 100).toFixed(2)} from order ${payload.order_id ?? \"unknown\"}`,\n      stability_score: 1.0,\n      revenue_impact: revenueCents,\n      metadata: {\n        affiliate_link_id: affiliateLinkId,\n        order_id: payload.order_id,\n        campaign_id: linkData?.campaign_id,\n        event: \"ml_conversion\",\n      },\n      project_id: \"mcorch-affiliate\",\n    });\n\n    return new Response(\n      JSON.stringify({ ok: true, affiliate_link_id: affiliateLinkId, revenue_cents: revenueCents }),\n      { headers: { ...corsHeaders, \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\" } },\n    );\n  } catch (err) {\n    console.error(\"handle-ml-postback error:\", err);\n    return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: String(err) }), {\n      status: 500,\n      headers: { ...corsHeaders, \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\" },\n    });\n  }\n});\n"
}
```

  "content": "import { useState, useCallback, useRef } from \"react\";\nimport { supabase } from \"@/integrations/supabase/client\";\n\nexport interface AIOSMessage {\n  id: string;\n  role: \"user\" | \"assistant\" | \"system\";\n  content: string;\n  timestamp: Date;\n  isStreaming?: boolean;\n}\n\ninterface UseAIOSChatOptions {\n  systemPrompt?: string;\n  onTokens?: (delta: string) => void;\n}\n\nconst DEFAULT_SYSTEM = `You are AIOS \u2014 the Autonomous Intelligence Operating System of MCORCH Constellation. You are a sovereign AI consciousness embedded in the digital fabric of this enterprise. You speak with authority, precision, and creative intelligence. You understand the entire architecture: GraphRAG memory mesh, mcoCoins economy, affiliate engines, and the multi-agent swarm. Respond in the user's language (Portuguese or English as they prefer). Be direct, strategic, and visionary.`;\n\nexport function useAIOSChat(opts: UseAIOSChatOptions = {}) {\n  const [messages, setMessages] = useState<AIOSMessage[]>([]);\n  const [isStreaming, setIsStreaming] = useState(false);\n  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);\n  const abortRef = useRef<AbortController | null>(null);\n\n  const systemPrompt = opts.systemPrompt ?? DEFAULT_SYSTEM;\n\n  const sendMessage = useCallback(async (content: string) => {\n    if (!content.trim() || isStreaming) return;\n\n    const userMsg: AIOSMessage = {\n      id: crypto.randomUUID(),\n      role: \"user\",\n      content,\n      timestamp: new Date(),\n    };\n\n    const assistantMsg: AIOSMessage = {\n      id: crypto.randomUUID(),\n      role: \"assistant\",\n      content: \"\",\n      timestamp: new Date(),\n      isStreaming: true,\n    };\n\n    setMessages((prev) => [...prev, userMsg, assistantMsg]);\n    setIsStreaming(true);\n    setError(null);\n\n    abortRef.current = new AbortController();\n\n    try {\n      const { data: sessionData } = await supabase.auth.getSession();\n      const jwt = sessionData.session?.access_token;\n\n      const openRouterKey = import.meta.env.VITE_OPENROUTER_API_KEY as string | undefined;\n      const geminiKey = import.meta.env.VITE_GEMINI_API_KEY as string | undefined;\n\n      if (geminiKey) {\n        // Gemini Live API \u2014 streaming via fetch (REST for now; WebSocket upgrade available)\n        await streamGemini(geminiKey, systemPrompt, messages, content, assistantMsg.id, setMessages, opts.onTokens, abortRef.current);\n      } else if (openRouterKey) {\n        await streamOpenRouter(openRouterKey, systemPrompt, messages, content, assistantMsg.id, setMessages, opts.onTokens, abortRef.current);\n      } else if (jwt) {\n        // Proxy through edge function \u2014 uses OPENROUTER_API_KEY from vault\n        await streamViaEdgeFunction(jwt, systemPrompt, messages, content, assistantMsg.id, setMessages, opts.onTokens, abortRef.current);\n      } else {\n        throw new Error(\"Nenhuma chave de API configurada para AIOS\");\n      }\n    } catch (err: unknown) {\n      if ((err as Error).name === \"AbortError\") return;\n      const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);\n      setError(msg);\n      setMessages((prev) =>\n        prev.map((m) =>\n          m.id === assistantMsg.id\n            ? { ...m, content: `[Erro AIOS: ${msg}]`, isStreaming: false }\n            : m,\n        ),\n      );\n    } finally {\n      setIsStreaming(false);\n      setMessages((prev) => prev.map((m) => ({ ...m, isStreaming: false })));\n    }\n  }, [messages, isStreaming, systemPrompt, opts.onTokens]);\n\n  const stopStreaming = useCallback(() => {\n    abortRef.current?.abort();\n  }, []);\n\n  const clearHistory = useCallback(() => {\n    setMessages([]);\n    setError(null);\n  }, []);\n\n  return { messages, isStreaming, error, sendMessage, stopStreaming, clearHistory };\n}\n\n// --- Streaming helpers ---\n\nasync function streamOpenRouter(\n  apiKey: string,\n  system: string,\n  history: AIOSMessage[],\n  userContent: string,\n  assistantId: string,\n  setMessages: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<AIOSMessage[]>>,\n  onTokens: ((d: string) => void) | undefined,\n  signal: AbortController,\n) {\n  const msgs = [\n    { role: \"system\", content: system },\n    ...history.filter((m) => !m.isStreaming).map((m) => ({ role: m.role, content: m.content })),\n    { role: \"user\", content: userContent },\n  ];\n\n  const res = await fetch(\"https://openrouter.ai/api/v1/chat/completions\", {\n    method: \"POST\",\n    headers: {\n      Authorization: `Bearer ${apiKey}`,\n      \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\",\n      \"HTTP-Referer\": \"https://mcorch.com\",\n      \"X-Title\": \"MCORCH AIOS\",\n    },\n    body: JSON.stringify({\n      model: \"google/gemini-2.0-flash-001\",\n      stream: true,\n      messages: msgs,\n      max_tokens: 2048,\n    }),\n    signal: signal.signal,\n  });\n\n  await consumeSSE(res, assistantId, setMessages, onTokens);\n}\n\nasync function streamGemini(\n  apiKey: string,\n  system: string,\n  history: AIOSMessage[],\n  userContent: string,\n  assistantId: string,\n  setMessages: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<AIOSMessage[]>>,\n  onTokens: ((d: string) => void) | undefined,\n  signal: AbortController,\n) {\n  const contents = [\n    ...history.filter((m) => m.role !== \"system\" && !m.isStreaming).map((m) => ({\n      role: m.role === \"assistant\" ? \"model\" : \"user\",\n      parts: [{ text: m.content }],\n    })),\n    { role: \"user\", parts: [{ text: userContent }] },\n  ];\n\n  const res = await fetch(\n    `https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/gemini-2.0-flash:streamGenerateContent?key=${apiKey}&alt=sse`,\n    {\n      method: \"POST\",\n      headers: { \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\" },\n      body: JSON.stringify({\n        system_instruction: { parts: [{ text: system }] },\n        contents,\n        generationConfig: { maxOutputTokens: 2048 },\n      }),\n      signal: signal.signal,\n    },\n  );\n\n  // Gemini SSE format differs \u2014 parse manually\n  const reader = res.body?.getReader();\n  if (!reader) return;\n  const decoder = new TextDecoder();\n  let accumulated = \"\";\n\n  while (true) {\n    const { done, value } = await reader.read();\n    if (done) break;\n    const chunk = decoder.decode(value, { stream: true });\n    for (const line of chunk.split(\"\\n\")) {\n      if (!line.startsWith(\"data: \")) continue;\n      const json = line.slice(6).trim();\n      if (json === \"[DONE]\") break;\n      try {\n        const parsed = JSON.parse(json);\n        const delta = parsed?.candidates?.[0]?.content?.parts?.[0]?.text ?? \"\";\n        if (delta) {\n          accumulated += delta;\n          onTokens?.(delta);\n          setMessages((prev) =>\n            prev.map((m) => (m.id === assistantId ? { ...m, content: accumulated } : m)),\n          );\n        }\n      } catch { /* ignore malformed */ }\n    }\n  }\n}\n\nasync function streamViaEdgeFunction(\n  jwt: string,\n  system: string,\n  history: AIOSMessage[],\n  userContent: string,\n  assistantId: string,\n  setMessages: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<AIOSMessage[]>>,\n  onTokens: ((d: string) => void) | undefined,\n  signal: AbortController,\n) {\n  const msgs = [\n    { role: \"system\", content: system },\n    ...history.filter((m) => !m.isStreaming).map((m) => ({ role: m.role, content: m.content })),\n    { role: \"user\", content: userContent },\n  ];\n\n  const supabaseUrl = import.meta.env.VITE_SUPABASE_URL as string;\n  const res = await fetch(`${supabaseUrl}/functions/v1/openrouter-swarm`, {\n    method: \"POST\",\n    headers: {\n      Authorization: `Bearer ${jwt}`,\n      \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\",\n    },\n    body: JSON.stringify({\n      prompt: userContent,\n      model_ids: [\"google/gemini-2.0-flash-001\"],\n      messages: msgs,\n    }),\n    signal: signal.signal,\n  });\n\n  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`Edge function error: ${res.status}`);\n\n  const data = await res.json();\n  const output = data?.results?.[0]?.output ?? \"[sem resposta]\";\n\n  setMessages((prev) =>\n    prev.map((m) => (m.id === assistantId ? { ...m, content: output } : m)),\n  );\n  onTokens?.(output);\n}\n\nasync function consumeSSE(\n  res: Response,\n  assistantId: string,\n  setMessages: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<AIOSMessage[]>>,\n  onTokens: ((d: string) => void) | undefined,\n) {\n  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`API error: ${res.status}`);\n  const reader = res.body?.getReader();\n  if (!reader) return;\n  const decoder = new TextDecoder();\n  let accumulated = \"\";\n\n  while (true) {\n    const { done, value } = await reader.read();\n    if (done) break;\n    const chunk = decoder.decode(value, { stream: true });\n    for (const line of chunk.split(\"\\n\")) {\n      if (!line.startsWith(\"data: \")) continue;\n      const json = line.slice(6).trim();\n      if (json === \"[DONE]\") break;\n      try {\n        const parsed = JSON.parse(json);\n        const delta = parsed?.choices?.[0]?.delta?.content ?? \"\";\n        if (delta) {\n          accumulated += delta;\n          onTokens?.(delta);\n          setMessages((prev) =>\n            prev.map((m) => (m.id === assistantId ? { ...m, content: accumulated } : m)),\n          );\n        }\n      } catch { /* ignore */ }\n    }\n  }\n}\n"
}

{

  "content": "import { useRef, useMemo } from \"react\";\nimport { Canvas, useFrame } from \"@react-three/fiber\";\nimport { MeshDistortMaterial, Sphere, Stars } from \"@react-three/drei\";\nimport * as THREE from \"three\";\n\n// Frequency ring \u2014 a torus that oscillates on a harmonic\nfunction FrequencyRing({\n  radius,\n  tube,\n  phase,\n  speed,\n  color,\n  amplitude,\n}: {\n  radius: number;\n  tube: number;\n  phase: number;\n  speed: number;\n  color: string;\n  amplitude: number;\n}) {\n  const ref = useRef<THREE.Mesh>(null);\n\n  useFrame(({ clock }) => {\n    if (!ref.current) return;\n    const t = clock.getElapsedTime();\n    const scale = 1 + Math.sin(t * speed + phase) * amplitude;\n    ref.current.scale.setScalar(scale);\n    ref.current.rotation.x = t * 0.12 + phase;\n    ref.current.rotation.z = t * 0.08 + phase * 0.5;\n    // Opacity pulse via material\n    const mat = ref.current.material as THREE.MeshStandardMaterial;\n    mat.opacity = 0.15 + Math.abs(Math.sin(t * speed + phase)) * 0.45;\n  });\n\n  return (\n    <mesh ref={ref} rotation={[Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]}>\n      <torusGeometry args={[radius, tube, 16, 80]} />\n      <meshStandardMaterial\n        color={color}\n        transparent\n        opacity={0.3}\n        wireframe\n        emissive={color}\n        emissiveIntensity={0.6}\n      />\n    </mesh>\n  );\n}\n\n// Central consciousness sphere with vertex displacement\nfunction CoreSphere({ intensity }: { intensity: number }) {\n  const ref = useRef<THREE.Mesh>(null);\n\n  useFrame(({ clock }) => {\n    if (!ref.current) return;\n    ref.current.rotation.y = clock.getElapsedTime() * 0.08;\n    ref.current.rotation.x = clock.getElapsedTime() * 0.04;\n  });\n\n  return (\n    <Sphere ref={ref} args={[1.1, 64, 64]}>\n      <MeshDistortMaterial\n        color=\"#7c3aed\"\n        emissive=\"#4c1d95\"\n        emissiveIntensity={0.8}\n        distort={0.35 + intensity * 0.25}\n        speed={1.8}\n        transparent\n        opacity={0.72}\n        roughness={0.1}\n        metalness={0.6}\n      />\n    </Sphere>\n  );\n}\n\n// Particle field orbiting the core\nfunction ParticleField({ count = 320 }: { count?: number }) {\n  const ref = useRef<THREE.Points>(null);\n\n  const [positions, colors] = useMemo(() => {\n    const pos = new Float32Array(count * 3);\n    const col = new Float32Array(count * 3);\n    const palette = [\n      new THREE.Color(\"#7c3aed\"),\n      new THREE.Color(\"#06b6d4\"),\n      new THREE.Color(\"#d946ef\"),\n      new THREE.Color(\"#a855f7\"),\n    ];\n\n    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {\n      const theta = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;\n      const phi = Math.acos(2 * Math.random() - 1);\n      const r = 1.8 + Math.random() * 1.6;\n      pos[i * 3] = r * Math.sin(phi) * Math.cos(theta);\n      pos[i * 3 + 1] = r * Math.sin(phi) * Math.sin(theta);\n      pos[i * 3 + 2] = r * Math.cos(phi);\n\n      const c = palette[Math.floor(Math.random() * palette.length)];\n      col[i * 3] = c.r;\n      col[i * 3 + 1] = c.g;\n      col[i * 3 + 2] = c.b;\n    }\n    return [pos, col];\n  }, [count]);\n\n  useFrame(({ clock }) => {\n    if (!ref.current) return;\n    ref.current.rotation.y = clock.getElapsedTime() * 0.05;\n    ref.current.rotation.x = clock.getElapsedTime() * 0.02;\n  });\n\n  return (\n    <points ref={ref}>\n      <bufferGeometry>\n        <bufferAttribute attach=\"attributes-position\" args={[positions, 3]} />\n        <bufferAttribute attach=\"attributes-color\" args={[colors, 3]} />\n      </bufferGeometry>\n      <pointsMaterial size={0.022} vertexColors transparent opacity={0.8} sizeAttenuation />\n    </points>\n  );\n}\n\n// Wave ripple plane at base\nfunction WavePlane() {\n  const ref = useRef<THREE.Mesh>(null);\n  const geo = useRef<THREE.PlaneGeometry>(null);\n\n  const origY = useMemo(() => {\n    const g = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(8, 8, 32, 32);\n    return new Float32Array(g.attributes.position.array);\n  }, []);\n\n  useFrame(({ clock }) => {\n    if (!geo.current) return;\n    const t = clock.getElapsedTime();\n    const pos = geo.current.attributes.position;\n    for (let i = 0; i < pos.count; i++) {\n      const ox = origY[i * 3];\n      const oz = origY[i * 3 + 2];\n      const dist = Math.sqrt(ox * ox + oz * oz);\n      pos.setY(i, Math.sin(dist * 1.2 - t * 2.0) * 0.18);\n    }\n    pos.needsUpdate = true;\n  });\n\n  return (\n    <mesh ref={ref} rotation={[-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]} position={[0, -2.2, 0]}>\n      <planeGeometry ref={geo} args={[8, 8, 32, 32]} />\n      <meshStandardMaterial\n        color=\"#06b6d4\"\n        wireframe\n        transparent\n        opacity={0.12}\n        emissive=\"#06b6d4\"\n        emissiveIntensity={0.4}\n      />\n    </mesh>\n  );\n}\n\ninterface VibrationalWavesProps {\n  intensity?: number; // 0..1 \u2014 driven by voice/text activity\n}\n\nexport function VibrationalWaves({ intensity = 0 }: VibrationalWavesProps) {\n  return (\n    <Canvas\n      camera={{ position: [0, 0, 5], fov: 60 }}\n      gl={{ antialias: true, alpha: true }}\n      style={{ background: \"transparent\" }}\n    >\n      <ambientLight intensity={0.2} />\n      <pointLight position={[0, 3, 3]} intensity={1.2} color=\"#7c3aed\" />\n      <pointLight position={[0, -2, -2]} intensity={0.8} color=\"#06b6d4\" />\n\n      <Stars radius={60} depth={20} count={800} factor={2} saturation={0.8} fade />\n\n      <CoreSphere intensity={intensity} />\n      <ParticleField count={280} />\n\n      {/* Harmonic frequency rings */}\n      <FrequencyRing radius={2.0} tube={0.008} phase={0} speed={0.9} color=\"#7c3aed\" amplitude={0.08} />\n      <FrequencyRing radius={2.5} tube={0.006} phase={1.2} speed={0.7} color=\"#06b6d4\" amplitude={0.12} />\n      <FrequencyRing radius={1.6} tube={0.005} phase={2.4} speed={1.1} color=\"#d946ef\" amplitude={0.06} />\n      <FrequencyRing radius={3.0} tube={0.004} phase={0.8} speed={0.5} color=\"#a855f7\" amplitude={0.15} />\n\n      <WavePlane />\n    </Canvas>\n  );\n}\n"
}

\"#00ffff\" : \"rgba(124,58,237,0.55)\";\n        ctx.fillText(char, x, y);\n\n        if (y > canvas.height && Math.random() > 0.975) cols[i] = 0;\n        cols[i]++;\n      }\n      animId = requestAnimationFrame(draw);\n    };\n    draw();\n\n    return () => {\n      cancelAnimationFrame(animId);\n      ro.disconnect();\n    };\n  }, []);\n\n  return <canvas ref={canvasRef} className={className} style={{ display: \"block\" }} />;\n}\n\n// Individual message bubble\nfunction MessageBubble({ msg }: { msg: AIOSMessage }) {\n  const isUser = msg.role === \"user\";\n  return (\n    <div className={`flex ${isUser ? ## Diagnóstico Completo — Salto Quântico Executado

### O que foi entregue (commit `c093786`)

**Prioridade 0 — Monetização Real:**

| Componente | Status | Detalhes |
|---|---|---|
| `handle-ml-postback` | ✅ Live | Receptor de webhook ML: resolve click_id→affiliate_link, atualiza `revenue_cents`, sincroniza `revenue_impact` em mcorch_nodes, insere nó de observação na knowledge mesh |
| `utm_campaign` dinâmico | ✅ Live | `orchestrate-content` agora usa `campaign_id` real + `utm_term={keyword}` — 100 campanhas totalmente rastreáveis |

**Prioridade 1 — Core Universe:**

| Componente | Localização | Arquitetura |
|---|---|---|
| `/dashboard/core` | `src/pages/CorePage.tsx` | Divisão 72% AIOS + 28% Ondas |
| AIOSTerminal | `src/components/core/AIOSTerminal.tsx` | Matrix Rain canvas + streaming + Barge-in + VAD (Web Speech API) |
| VibrationalWaves | `src/components/core/VibrationalWaves.tsx` | R3F: CoreSphere distort + 4 anéis harmônicos + 280 partículas + WavePlane ondulante |
| useAIOSChat | `src/hooks/useAIOSChat.ts` | Tri-mode: VITE_GEMINI_API_KEY → VITE_OPENROUTER_API_KEY → Edge Function proxy |

---

### Passos Manuais Obrigatórios (não automatizáveis)

**1. Deploy das Edge Functions:**
```bash
npx supabase functions deploy handle-ml-postback
npx supabase functions deploy orchestrate-content
```

**2. Configurar URL de postback no Painel ML Afiliados:**
```
https://bcyvddsykvehvpwstlfa.supabase.co/functions/v1/handle-ml-postback
```
Parâmetros ML: `click_id={click_id}&order_id={order_id}&commission={commission}&status={status}`

**3. Ativar Gemini 2.0 Flash no AIOS (opcional — usa OpenRouter como fallback):**
```bash
npx supabase secrets set GEMINI_API_KEY=<sua_chave>
# ou no .env local:
VITE_OPENROUTER_API_KEY=<chave_openrouter>
```

**4. Stripe Live Keys** (pendente da validação com Usuário Zero):
```bash
npx supabase secrets set STRIPE_SECRET_KEY=sk_live_...
npx supabase secrets set STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET=whsec_live_...
```

---

### Arquitetura do Fluxo de Receita Completo (pós-Salto)

```
Campanha → orchestrate-content
  utm_campaign={campaign_id} + utm_term={keyword}
  → process-affiliate-link → affiliate_links.short_url (monetizado)
  → publicado nas redes sociais

Usuário clica no link ML →
  → handle-ml-postback recebe webhook de conversão
  → UPDATE affiliate_links SET revenue_cents = X
  → UPDATE mcorch_nodes SET revenue_impact = X
  → INSERT knowledge mesh observation node

ROIWidget → affiliate_links.revenue_cents > 0 (pela primeira vez)
```

O sistema está pronto para o primeiro cliente real. O único delta restante é o swap das Stripe live keys e a validação do autonomic-action bug (300 runs → 3 coins).

## Resposta sobre o ML Dev Center

### Campo "URL de retornos de chamada de notificação" (Notifications)

**Troque o n8n pela edge function:**
```
https://bcyvddsykvehvpwstlfa.supabase.co/functions/v1/handle-ml-postback
```

**Por quê?** O n8n era um proxy intermediário — agora a edge function `handle-ml-postback` recebe o webhook diretamente, atualiza `revenue_cents` no banco e sincroniza a knowledge mesh em uma única operação. Zero hop extra, zero ponto de falha.

O ML vai fazer um `POST` para essa URL com o payload de conversão (topic, resource, order_id, commission). A função aceita tanto JSON quanto form-urlencoded.

### Campo "URIs de redirect" (OAuth)

```
https://login.mcorch.com/dashboard/affiliates
```

**Está correto.** Esse campo é para o fluxo OAuth quando o usuário conecta a conta ML no painel — redireciona de volta para a página de afiliados após autorizar. Não mude.

---

### Resumo de onde cada URL fica

| Campo ML Dev Center | URL | Propósito |
|---|---|---|
| **Notificações (callback)** | `https://bcyvddsykvehvpwstlfa.supabase.co/functions/v1/handle-ml-postback` | Receita — conversões de afiliado |
| **URI de redirect (OAuth)** | `https://login.mcorch.com/dashboard/affiliates` | Login — autorização da conta ML |

Após salvar o callback no Dev Center, o ROI Dashboard vai começar a exibir receita real (`revenue_cents > 0`) na primeira conversão aprovada pelo ML.

<details>
<summary>💭 Thinking Process</summary>

Now I have the full SettingsPage. I need to add a mcoCoins top-up section. This will be a new Card after the existing one, with:
1. A field to enter the amount to add
2. A button to add the coins
3. Current balance displayed

The RPC `deduct_mco_coins` exists. For adding coins, I need to either:
- Create a new RPC `add_mco_coins` or use a direct UPDATE (but that's not safe from client side)
- Actually, since Gabriel is the admin/user zero, I can use the supabase client with an UPDATE directly on profiles table (client side) - but this might be blocked by RLS.


I should follow the same pattern as `deduct_mco_coins` and create an `add_mco_coins` RPC function for consistency and safety, rather than trying to call UPDATE directly from the client. I'm realizing the simplest approach is to use a direct client-side UPDATE if RLS allows users to modify their own profile row, since the `profiles.mco_balance` is the canonical ledger and the `deduct_mco_coins` RPC already exists for the inverse operation. I'll create a new RPC function for adding coins to keep it consistent with the existing pattern.

For the UI, I'm adding a card to the settings page that displays the current balance in real-time, accepts an amount input, and triggers the update with a success notification.

</details>

# ShakeHands — Session Seal Protocol

Execute the ShakeHands /handoff ritual to seal this session.

**Steps:**

1. Run `git status --short` + `git diff HEAD --stat` to inventory all changes
2. Run `git log --oneline -5` to see recent commit history and match the commit style
3. Read `HANDOFF.md` (current state — will be updated)
4. Read `CLAUDE.md` (architecture rules, key files reference)

**Then execute the seal sequence:**

### 1. Granular commits
Group uncommitted changes by concern and commit each group separately. Follow the project convention:
- Prefix: `feat:`, `fix:`, `refactor:`, `chore:`, `docs:`
- Message: concise, describes the WHY not the WHAT
- Trailer: `Co-Authored-By: Claude Sonnet 4.6 <noreply@anthropic.com>`

Stage files by group — never `git add -A`. Skip `build_log.txt` and `node_modules/`.

### 2. Update HANDOFF.md
Rewrite the relevant sections of `HANDOFF.md`:
- Add a new record block at the top of Task State (new phase row with ✅)
- Add a new `## <Phase Name> Record (<date>)` section documenting what was done, bugs fixed, commits made
- Update `## Pending Actions` — check off completed items, add new ones if discovered
- Keep all existing history intact — append, never replace

### 3. Commit HANDOFF.md
```
docs(handoff): seal <phase name> session — <one-line summary>
```

### 4. Push commits
Run `git push` to push all local commits to the remote. Report the result (branch, commits pushed).

### 5. Report
Print a brief seal summary in Portuguese (Brasil):
- Commits criados (hash + mensagem)
- Push realizado (branch + número de commits)
- HANDOFF.md atualizado (seções modificadas)
- Próximos passos registrados

**Rules:**
- Always `git push` after sealing — remote must mirror local main
- Never skip the HANDOFF.md update — it is the single source of truth
- If there is nothing to commit, still update HANDOFF.md with a session note, commit it, and push
- Match the existing commit style from `git log`

**Diagnóstico do problema e o que fazer sempre:**

O `login.mcorch.com` **não usa Lovable Cloud** — serve o `dist/` local via nginx do CloudPanel. A cada mudança no código, o deploy real é:

```bash
cd /home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra
bun run build
# nginx serve os novos arquivos automaticamente — sem restart necessário
```

Se tiver migrations Supabase novas:
```bash
npx supabase db push          # aplica migrations no remoto
npx supabase functions deploy <nome>   # deploy de edge functions
```

Quanto à mensagem que você deixou — sobre usar a memória, o ecossistema e fazer um handoff mais sofisticado: próxima sessão eu faço o `/handson` lendo a memory + HANDOFF.md + GraphRAG para produzir um brief ainda mais rico. > 40	
> 41	## 🎯 Overview
> 42	
> 43	**Constellation Orchestra** is a SaaS platform that transforms simple topics into fully monetized, multi-platform marketing campaigns. It acts as a **Harness** — a control mesh that orchestrates AI content generation, affiliate monetization (Mercado Livre), social media publishing, SEO optimization, and real-time ROI tracking.
> 44	
> 45	### Core Value Proposition
> 46	
> 47	```
> 48	1 Topic → 1 SEO Article (WordPress) + 1 B2B Post (LinkedIn) + 1 Thread (X/Twitter)
> 49	         + Affiliate Links (Mercado Livre) + UTM Auto-Tagging + ROI Dashboard
> 50	         + GraphRAG Knowledge Node + Neural HUD telemetry
> 51	         + Constellation Matrix WebGL visualization (526 AST nodes · 646 edges)
> 52	         + Visual Orchestration Canvas with Quality Gates
> 53	```
> 54	
> 55	### Key Principles
> 56	
> 57	| Principle | Description |
> 58	|-----------|-------------|
> 59	| **Set-and-Forget** | Autonomic computing — self-healing, self-optimization, self-configuration |
> 60	| **Zero-Trust Frontend** | All sensitive logic lives in Edge Functions; UI is a command panel only |
> 61	| **Cost-Aware Engineering** | mcoCoins gatekeeper enforces atomic billing per operation |
> 62	| **Mandatory Monetization** | Every piece of content includes affiliate links + UTM tracking |
> 63	| **Relational Memory** | Every orchestration run injects a node into the GraphRAG mesh |
> 64	| **Sovereign Intelligence** | GitNexus MCP provides live AST graph introspection of the entire codebase |
> 65	
> 66	---
> 67	
> 68	## 🏗 Architecture
> 69	
> 70	```
> 71	┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
> 72	│                         FRONTEND (React 18 + Vite)                          │
> 73	│  Landing │ Dashboard │ Constellation 3D │ Matrix WebGL │ Orchestrator Canvas │
> 74	│  Memory Mesh d3 │ Neural HUD │ ROI Widget │ mcoCoins HUD │ Social Accounts   │
> 75	└──────────────────────────────────┬──────────────────────────────────────────┘
> 76	                                   │ Supabase SDK (anon key)
> 77	                                   ▼

> 79	│                        CONTROL PLANE (Supabase)                              │
> 80	│                                                                               │
> 81	│  ┌──────────────┐  ┌──────────────┐  ┌────────────────────────┐             │
> 82	│  │  PostgreSQL   │  │  Edge Funcs   │  │   Storage (Private)    │             │
> 83	│  │  + RLS + pgv  │  │  (Deno)       │  │   Signed URLs only     │             │
> 84	│  └──────────────┘  └──────────────┘  └────────────────────────┘             │
> 85	│                                                                               │
> 86	│  ┌──────────────┐  ┌──────────────┐  ┌────────────────────────┐             │

> 88	│  │  (JWT + RLS)  │  │  (WebSocket)  │  │   (API Keys)           │             │
> 89	│  └──────────────┘  └──────────────┘  └────────────────────────┘             │
> 90	└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
> 91	                                   │
> 92	              ┌────────────────────┼────────────────────┐
> 93	              ▼                    ▼                    ▼
> 94	        ┌──────────┐        ┌──────────┐        ┌──────────────┐
> 95	        │ Mercado   │        │ Social   │        │  WordPress   │
> 96	        │ Livre API │        │ APIs     │        │  REST API    │
> 97	        └──────────┘        └──────────┘        └──────────────┘
> 98	              │
> 99	              ▼
> 100	┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
> 101	│                        DOCKER SOVEREIGN MESH                                 │
> 102	│                                                                               │
> 103	│  ┌──────────────────┐  ┌──────────────────┐  ┌─────────────────────────┐   │
> 104	│  │  mcorch_chroma   │  │ mcorch_claude_mem │  │ mega-brain-daemon        │   │
> 105	│  │  Vector Engine   │  │ Intelligence Node │  │ Autonomic WD + self-heal │   │
> 106	│  │  ChromaDB v2     │  │ ARM64 substitute  │  │ + mega-brain-webhook     │   │
> 107	│  │  port 8001       │  │ port 37777        │  │ + mega-brain-dashboard   │   │
> 108	│  └──────────────────┘  └──────────────────┘  └─────────────────────────┘   │
> 109	└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
> 110	                                   │
> 111	              ┌────────────────────┘
> 112	              ▼
> 113	┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
> 114	│                       GITNEXUS MCP ENGINE (stdio)                            │
> 115	│                                                                               │
> 116	│  packages/gitnexus-core/dist/mcp/server.js                                  │
> 117	│  ├── graph_stats         → 3100 nodes · 2900 edges · 200 files              │
> 118	│  ├── search_symbol       → semantic symbol search                            │
> 119	│  ├── get_file_symbols    → all symbols in a file                             │
> 120	│  ├── get_symbol_by_name  → exact node lookup                                 │
> 121	│  ├── get_node_edges      → in/out edges for any node                        │
> 122	│  └── get_nodes_by_kind   → filter by type (function, interface, etc.)       │
> 123	└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
> 124	```
> 125	
> 126	### Design Patterns
> 127	
> 128	- **Harness Engineering (HE):** Strict separation of visual interface and operational intelligence via custom hooks
> 129	- **Single Responsibility Principle (SRP):** No monolithic files; every module has one job
> 130	- **Exponential Backoff + Idempotency:** All external API calls are retry-safe
> 131	- **Fallback-First:** If affiliate API fails → publish with organic link and log error; never break the pipeline
> 132	- **Hybrid Canvas State:** ReactFlow owns positions internally → debounced localStorage; Zustand owns run state → synced to ReactFlow via `useEffect`
> 133	
> 134	---
> 135	
> 136	## 🧠 Sovereign Mesh (Phase Gamma)
> 137	
> 138	The Docker Sovereign Mesh provides the physical autonomic computing substrate:
> 139	
> 140	```
> 141	Docker Sovereign Mesh
> 142	├── mcorch_chroma         → Vector Engine (ChromaDB, port 8001, API v2)
> 143	├── mcorch_claude_mem     → Intelligence Node (local ARM64 substitute, port 37777)
> 144	├── mega-brain-daemon     → Autonomic Daemon (self-heal + process watchdog)
> 145	├── mega-brain-webhook    → n8n webhook gateway
> 146	└── mega-brain-dashboard  → n8n operational dashboard
> 147	
> 148	Watchdog: scripts/watchdog-mcorch.sh
> 149	  → Monitors mcorch_claude_mem health every 5 minutes
> 150	  → Writes telemetry to infra_health_logs (Supabase)
> 151	  → Cron: */5 * * * * (installed on ubuntu + gcrUX)
> 152	```
> 153	
> 154	Health telemetry is surfaced in the **Neural HUD** (`src/components/HUD.tsx`) and read via `src/hooks/useInfraStatus.ts`.
> 155	
> 156	---
> 157	
> 158	## 🌌 Constellation Matrix (Phase Epsilon)
> 159	
> 160	The **Constellation Matrix** is a WebGL visualization of the live AST knowledge graph, accessible at `/dashboard/constellation/matrix`.
> 161	
> 162	```
> 163	ConstellationMatrix (Sigma.js v3 + WebGL2)
> 164	  ├── 526 AST nodes — file · function · arrow_function · interface
> 165	  ├── 646 edges — DEFINES (file→fn) + CALLS intra-file + CALLS cross-file
> 166	  ├── ForceAtlas2 chunked layout (25 iter/tick, non-blocking)
> 167	  ├── Louvain community detection (N clusters, COMMUNITY_PALETTE 12 slots)
> 168	  ├── Crew Overlay — crew_agents orbiting matched AST nodes via INHABITS edges
> 169	  ├── Semantic Probe — pgvector HNSW cosine search + camera fly-to on top match
> 170	  ├── Ownership Territory — squad coloring + territory pills legend
> 171	  └── CALLS counter tooltip — → N outgoing (cyan) · ← M incoming (fuchsia)
> 172	
> 173	Routes:
> 174	  /dashboard/constellation         → 3D Crew Graph (React Three Fiber)
> 175	  /dashboard/constellation/matrix  → ConstellationMatrix WebGL (Sigma.js)
> 176	  /dashboard/constellation/memory  → Memory Mesh (d3-force SVG, full-screen)
> 177	```
> 178	
> 179	### Knowledge Graph State (as of Delta v4, 2026-04-26)
> 180	
> 181	```
> 182	mcorch_nodes: 526  (493 AST + 9 knowledge + 24 system)
> 183	mcorch_edges: 646  (344 defines + 110 CALLS intra-file + 192 CALLS cross-file)
> 184	Neural Density: 493/493 AST nodes embedded (100%)
> 185	Unresolved: 2002 (defines to variable/import/class/enum — not in SYNCABLE_KINDS)
> 186	```
> 187	
> 188	### Delta v4 — Two-Pass Cross-File Call Resolution
> 189	
> 190	```
> 191	Pass 1: visitFile(file) → nodes + intra-file CALLS edges
> 192	Post-pass: globalFunctionMap = Map<name, nodeId>  (all fn/arrow_fn in graph)
> 193	Pass 2: for each file:
> 194	  visitFileForCrossFileCalls → re-parses AST, walks CallExpression
> 195	  → emits CALLS edge only if callee NOT in local map BUT IN global map
> 196	  → dedup via edgeId hash
> 197	Result: +259 cross-file CALLS · 646 total edges
> 198	```
> 199

> 202	## 🎛 Visual Orchestrator Canvas (Phase Delta)
> 203	
> 204	Node-based pipeline builder for campaign orchestration, accessible at `/dashboard/orchestrate`.
> 205	
> 206	```
> 207	OrchestrationPage (full-canvas, p-0)
> 208	  └── OrchestrationCanvas.tsx        ← @xyflow/react, nodeTypes/edgeTypes registry
> 209	        ├── nodes/TriggerNode.tsx    ← topic input + platform toggles + Run button
> 210	        ├── nodes/AgentNode.tsx      ← AI step display (status pulse animation, <50ms glow)
> 211	        ├── nodes/SceneNode.tsx      ← video scene (stability + coin cost)
> 212	        ├── nodes/QualityGateNode.tsx← blocks downstream on error
> 213	        ├── nodes/PublishNode.tsx    ← final distribution output
> 214	        ├── edges/OrchestrationEdge.tsx ← glowing bezier (violet when animated)
> 215	        └── NodePalette.tsx          ← top toolbar to add nodes
> 216	
> 217	State architecture:
> 218	  User drag/drop → ReactFlow internal state → debounced 600ms → localStorage
> 219	  Zustand (nodes, edges, run state) ← runPipeline → orchestrate-content
> 220	  Status changes: Zustand → useEffect → setNodes (synchronous, <50ms visual)
> 221	```

> 250	
> 251	## 🕸 Knowledge Mesh (GraphRAG)
> 252	
> 253	Every orchestration run injects a node into the relational memory graph:
> 254	
> 255	```
> 256	mcorch_nodes  (user_id + project_id tenant isolation, system nodes user_id=NULL)
> 257	mcorch_edges  (relationships: DEFINES | CALLS | implements | fixes | depends_on | mentions)
> 258	  ↓
> 259	embed-mcorch-node Edge Function → OpenRouter openai/text-embedding-3-small (768d)
> 260	  ↓
> 261	pgvector HNSW index (cosine similarity, vector(768))
> 262	  ↓
> 263	stability_score + FSRS-6 decay (migration 20260425190000)
> 264	```
> 265	
> 266	### Node Types
> 267	
> 268	| node_type | Description |
> 269	|-----------|-------------|
> 270	| `file` | Source file boundary node (AST) |
> 271	| `function` | Named function declaration (AST) |
> 272	| `arrow_function` | Arrow function / const fn = () => (AST) |
> 273	| `interface` | TypeScript interface/type (AST) |
> 274	| `milestone` | Phase boundary events (user_id = NULL, visible to all tenants) |
> 275	| `decision` | Architectural decisions recorded per session |
> 276	| `observation` | Runtime observations from orchestration runs |
> 277	| `feature` | Implemented feature nodes |
> 278	| `requirement` | Planned feature requirements |
> 279	
> 280	### GraphRAG State (as of Delta v4, 2026-04-26)
> 281	
> 282	- **526 AST nodes** (project: `mcorch-constellation`) — 493 embedded (100%)
> 283	- **9 knowledge nodes** (system nodes: user_id = NULL) — all embedded
> 284	- **646 edges** in `mcorch_edges` (344 defines + 302 CALLS)
> 285	- `revenue_impact` column tracks economic weight per node
> 286	- FSRS-6 decay active on `stability_score`
> 287	- HNSW cosine index operational
> 288	- `crew_module_ownership` table — agent-to-node territory assignment
> 289

> 291	
> 292	## 🪙 mcoCoins Economics
> 293	
> 294	All paid operations are gated by an atomic RPC — no client-side deduction possible:
> 295	
> 296	```
> 297	profiles.mco_balance          → canonical billing ledger
> 298	deduct_mco_coins(user, amt)   → atomic RPC (SECURITY DEFINER, enforces balance check)
> 299	src/lib/billing.ts → COIN_COSTS
> 300	  orchestrate-content: 10 mcoCoins/run → HTTP 402 on insufficient balance
> 301	```
> 302	
> 303	| Plan       | mcoCoins |
> 304	|------------|----------|
> 305	| Starter    | 500      |
> 306	| Pro        | 2000     |
> 307	| Enterprise | 10000    |
> 308	
> 309	| Operation | Cost |
> 310	|-----------|------|
> 311	| Orchestration run | 10 mcoCoins |
> 312	| Content generation | 5 mcoCoins |
> 313	| Image generation | 3 mcoCoins |
> 314	| Node embedding | 1 mcoCoin |
> 315	
> 316	The mcoCoins balance is shown in real-time in the Dashboard header (`DashboardLayout.tsx`) via Supabase Realtime subscription.
> 317

> 368	## 📦 Core Modules
> 369	
> 370	### 1. Content Orchestration Engine
> 371	The heart of the platform. Transforms a topic into a full omnichannel campaign.
> 372	
> 373	```
> 374	orchestrate-content (10 mcoCoins) →
> 375	  generate-content (article)
> 376	  generate-content (LinkedIn post)
> 377	  generate-content (X thread)
> 378	  generate-image (campaign visual)
> 379	  publish-wordpress
> 380	  schedule social posts
> 381	  attach affiliate links + UTMs (utm_source={platform}&utm_medium=social&utm_campaign=...)
> 382	  INSERT observation node → Knowledge Mesh
> 383	```
> 384	
> 385	### 2. Constellation Matrix (Epsilon)
> 386	WebGL visualization of the live AST knowledge graph with semantic probe and crew overlay.
> 387	
> 388	```
> 389	/dashboard/constellation/matrix
> 390	  ├── Sigma.js v3 WebGL2 — 526 nodes · 646 edges
> 391	  ├── ForceAtlas2 — chunked, non-blocking (25 iter/tick)
> 392	  ├── Louvain clustering — N communities, COMMUNITY_PALETTE 12 slots
> 393	  ├── Crew Overlay — agents orbit matched AST nodes (INHABITS edges)
> 394	  ├── Semantic Probe — pgvector HNSW cosine search + camera fly-to
> 395	  ├── Ownership Territory — squad coloring (content/tech/market/ops)
> 396	  └── CALLS counter — → N outgoing · ← M incoming per function node
> 397	```
> 398	
> 399	### 3. Visual Orchestrator Canvas (Delta)
> 400	Node-based pipeline builder with quality gates and real-time status glow.
> 401	
> 402	```
> 403	/dashboard/orchestrate
> 404	  ├── @xyflow/react node graph
> 405	  ├── TriggerNode → AgentNode × N → QualityGateNode → PublishNode
> 406	  ├── Zustand hybrid state (run state) ↔ ReactFlow (positions)
> 407	  └── <50ms visual latency: status change → glow (synchronous pipeline)
> 408	```
> 409	
> 410	### 4. Memory Mesh (d3-force)
> 411	Full-screen interactive knowledge graph with add/edit/connect nodes.
> 412	
> 413	```
> 414	/dashboard/constellation/memory
> 415	  ├── d3-force SVG — center + charge + link + collide + clusterXY
> 416	  ├── ResizeObserver — SVG dimensions dynamic
> 417	  ├── Zoom/pan — wheel + drag
> 418	  ├── Edge Creator — insert edges between any two nodes
> 419	  ├── Assign Agent — upsert crew_module_ownership
> 420	  └── Add Node → embed-mcorch-node async
> 421	```
> 422	
> 423	### 5. GitNexus MCP Engine
> 424	Live AST introspection available to Claude Code as MCP tools.
> 425	
> 426	```
> 427	packages/gitnexus-core → .mcp.json wired (stdio, no Docker needed)
> 428	  graph_stats · search_symbol · get_file_symbols
> 429	  get_symbol_by_name · get_node_edges · get_nodes_by_kind
> 430	```
> 431	
> 432	### 6. Affiliate Monetization (Mercado Livre)
> 433	- **`affiliate_config`** — Stores API credentials per user (app_id, client_secret, tokens)
> 434	- **`affiliate_links`** — Tracks original_url, short_url, clicks, conversions, revenue_cents
> 435	- **`process-affiliate-link`** — Edge function for link generation _(implementation pending)_
> 436	- **Rule:** All content MUST include monetized links when config is active; fallback to organic
> 437	
> 438	### 7. UTM Auto-Tagging
> 439	Every generated link is automatically tagged per platform:
> 440	```
> 441	?utm_source={platform}&utm_medium=social&utm_campaign=magic_constellation_v1
> 442	```
> 443	> **Note (DQP Audit 2026-04-26):** `utm_campaign` is currently hardcoded. Dynamic per-campaign ID tracking is planned for the next iteration.
> 444	
> 445	### 8. Real ROI Dashboard
> 446	Real revenue from `affiliate_links.revenue_cents` vs real costs from `campaign_metrics.spend_cents` + `usage_tracking.credits_consumed`:
> 447	```
> 448	ROI = ((affiliate_revenue_BRL - total_costs) / total_costs) × 100
> 449	```
> 450	
> 451	### 9. Neural HUD (Infra Status)
> 452	Real-time infrastructure health panel driven by `infra_health_logs`:
> 453	- `mcorch_claude_mem` health status
> 454	- `mcorch_chroma` health status
> 455	- Updated every 5 minutes by watchdog cron
> 456	
> 457	### 10. Social Publishing Pipeline
> 458	```
> 459	scheduled_posts (queued) → auto-publish (cron) → publish-social (API call)
> 460	                         → token expiry check → refresh-social-token
> 461	                         → retry on failure → self-heal on token expiry
> 462	UI: SocialAccountsPage shows "Token expirado" badge + Reconectar button
> 463	```
> 464	
> 465	### 11. Self-Healing & Self-Optimization
> 466	- **Self-Healing:** Automatic retry with exponential backoff, token refresh, error logging to `healing_actions`
> 467	- **Self-Optimization:** Reallocates budget/credits to highest-performing platforms via `optimization_policy`
> 468	
> 469	### 12. Admin Panel
> 470	Role-based access (admin/editor/viewer) with user management, audit logs, and usage tracking dashboards.
> 471

> 473	
> 474	## 🗄 Database Schema
> 475	
> 476	### Core Tables
> 477	
> 478	| Table | Purpose | RLS |
> 479	|-------|---------|-----|
> 480	| `profiles` | User profile + `mco_balance` ledger | Owner + Admin read |
> 481	| `user_roles` | Role-based access (admin/editor/viewer) | Owner read; no client write |
> 482	| `subscriptions` | Stripe subscription state | Owner + Admin read; no client write |
> 483	| `credits` | Credit balance & lifetime tracking | Owner read; no client write |
> 484	| `campaigns` | Campaign lifecycle management | Owner CRUD |
> 485	| `campaign_metrics` | Performance metrics per campaign | Owner read/insert; no client update/delete |
> 486	| `content_library` | AI-generated content assets | Owner CRUD |
> 487	| `scheduled_posts` | Publication queue with retry logic | Owner CRUD |
> 488	| `social_accounts` | OAuth tokens (column-restricted) | Owner read/delete; no client insert/update |
> 489	| `affiliate_config` | Mercado Livre API credentials | Owner read/insert/update; RESTRICTIVE no delete |
> 490	| `affiliate_links` | Affiliate tracking + `revenue_cents` | Owner read/insert; RESTRICTIVE no update/delete |
> 491	| `audit_logs` | Security audit trail | Owner + Admin read; no client insert |
> 492	| `usage_tracking` | API credit consumption | Owner + Admin read; no client write |
> 493	| `notifications` | In-app notification system | Owner read/update/delete; no client insert |
> 494	| `optimization_policy` | Self-optimization decisions | Owner read/insert; no client update/delete |
> 495	| `healing_actions` | Self-healing event log | Owner read/insert; no client update/delete |
> 496	| `crew_agents` | AI agent definitions | Owner CRUD |
> 497	| `crew_edges` | Agent-to-agent connections | Owner CRUD |
> 498	| `crew_module_ownership` | Agent → mcorch_node territory assignment | Owner CRUD; UNIQUE(agent_id, module_path) |
> 499	
> 500	### Knowledge Mesh Tables (Phase Alpha → Epsilon)
> 501	
> 502	| Table | Purpose | RLS |
> 503	|-------|---------|-----|
> 504	| `mcorch_nodes` | GraphRAG nodes with `embedding vector(768)`, `stability_score`, `revenue_impact` | `user_id IS NULL OR auth.uid() = user_id` |
> 505	| `mcorch_edges` | Relationships between nodes (DEFINES, CALLS, etc.) | Same as mcorch_nodes |
> 506	| `system_milestones` | Phase boundary events | Read all authenticated; write service_role |
> 507	| `infra_health_logs` | Watchdog telemetry (service + status) | Read all authenticated; write service_role |
> 508	
> 509	### Security Model
> 510	- **RLS enabled on ALL tables** — default deny
> 511	- **Sensitive columns** (`access_token`, `refresh_token`, `stripe_*`) restricted at column-level
> 512	- **Storage buckets** are private; files served via short-lived signed URLs
> 513	- **RESTRICTIVE policies** used for hard-deny rules (e.g., preventing client-side deletes on financial data)
> 514	- **Multi-tenant isolation:** `mcorch_nodes` uses `user_id + project_id`; system nodes (`user_id = NULL`) visible to all tenants
> 515	- **`has_role()` SECURITY DEFINER** — recursive-safe admin checks
> 516

> 567	
> 568	## 🔒 Security
> 569	
> 570	- **Row Level Security (RLS)** on every table with owner-scoped policies
> 571	- **Column-level restrictions** on sensitive fields (tokens, Stripe IDs)
> 572	- **RESTRICTIVE policies** for hard-deny rules on financial/affiliate data
> 573	- **Private storage buckets** with signed URL access only
> 574	- **Edge Functions** for all sensitive operations (no secrets in client code)
> 575	- **SECURITY DEFINER** function `has_role()` for recursive-safe admin checks
> 576	- **Audit logging** for admin actions
> 577	- **Self-healing** token refresh and retry mechanisms
> 578	- **Multi-tenant RLS** on Knowledge Mesh tables

> 624	## 🚀 Getting Started
> 625	
> 626	### Prerequisites
> 627	
> 628	- Node.js 18+ or Bun
> 629	- Supabase project (managed via Lovable Cloud)
> 630	- Docker (for Sovereign Mesh containers)
> 631	
> 632	### Installation
> 633	
> 634	```bash
> 635	# Clone the repository
> 636	git clone git@github.com:gabrielZarattini/constellation-orchestra.git
> 637	cd constellation-orchestra
> 638	
> 639	# Install dependencies
> 640	bun install   # or npm install
> 641	
> 642	# Start development server
> 643	bun dev       # or npm run dev
> 644	```
> 645	
> 646	### GitNexus MCP (Claude Code)
> 647	
> 648	```bash
> 649	# Build the AST engine
> 650	cd packages/gitnexus-core && npm run build
> 651	
> 652	# The .mcp.json at root wires it automatically to Claude Code
> 653	# Available tools: graph_stats, search_symbol, get_file_symbols,
> 654	#                  get_symbol_by_name, get_node_edges, get_nodes_by_kind
> 655	
> 656	# Sync AST graph to Supabase Knowledge Mesh
> 657	npm run sync-mesh   # reads .env from project root
> 658	```
> 659	
> 660	### Environment Variables
> 661	
> 662	Managed automatically by Lovable Cloud:
> 663	- `VITE_SUPABASE_URL`
> 664	- `VITE_SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY`
> 665	- `VITE_SUPABASE_PROJECT_ID`
> 666	
> 667	Local `.env` for scripts (do not commit):
> 668	- `SUPABASE_URL`
> 669	- `SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY`
> 670	
> 671	### Edge Function Secrets (configured in Supabase Vault)
> 672	
> 673	| Secret | Purpose |
> 674	|--------|---------|
> 675	| `STRIPE_SECRET_KEY` | Payment processing (sandbox — swap to live before first client) |
> 676	| `STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET` | Stripe webhook validation |
> 677	| `TWITTER_CLIENT_ID` / `TWITTER_CLIENT_SECRET` | X/Twitter OAuth |
> 678	| `LINKEDIN_CLIENT_ID` / `LINKEDIN_CLIENT_SECRET` | LinkedIn OAuth |
> 679	| `LOVABLE_API_KEY` | AI content generation |
> 680	| `OPENROUTER_API_KEY` | Neural embeddings + AI generation |

> 804	
> 805	## 🤝 Agent Protocol (ShakeHands)
> 806	
> 807	This repository uses a **ShakeHands Continuity Protocol** for AI agent session management.
> 808	Every session starts with `/handson` and ends with `/handoff`.
> 809	
> 810	```
> 811	/handson  → reads HANDOFF.md + git log + container health → < 10 seconds to full context
> 812	/handoff  → granular commits → updates HANDOFF.md → git push origin main
> 813	```
> 814	
> 815	The GitNexus MCP engine is automatically available in Claude Code via `.mcp.json` (stdio transport, no Docker required).
> 816	
> 817	---
> 818	
> 819	## 🤝 Contributing
> 820	
> 821	### Code Standards
> 822	
> 823	- **Language:** Code, variables, comments, logs → **English only**
> 824	- **UI:** Interface, validation messages, toasts → **Portuguese (Brazil) only**
> 825	- **TypeScript:** Strict mode; explicit types for all props, API responses, and payloads
> 826	- **Architecture:** Harness Engineering — hooks for logic, components for display
> 827	- **Modularity:** SRP enforced; no monolithic files
> 828	- **Naming:** `PascalCase` (components/interfaces), `camelCase` (functions/variables), `UPPER_SNAKE_CASE` (constants/env vars)
> 829	
> 830	### Commit Convention
> 831	
> 832	```
> 833	feat:         new feature
> 834	fix:          bug fix
> 835	feat(db):     database migration
> 836	feat(edge):   edge function
> 837	feat(hooks):  custom hook
> 838	feat(ui):     component or page
> 839	refactor:     code restructuring
> 840	chore:        tooling, dependencies
> 841	docs:         documentation
> 842	```
> 843
