                      GITNEXUS MCP ENGINE (stdio)                                 \u2502\n\u2502  packages/gitnexus-core/dist/mcp/server.js                                       \u2502\n\u2502  \u251c\u2500\u2500 graph_stats         \u2192 3100 nodes \u00b7 2900 edges \u00b7 200 files                  \u2502\n\u2502  \u251c\u2500\u2500 search_symbol       \u2192 semantic symbol search                                 \u2502\n\u2502  \u251c\u2500\u2500 get_file_symbols    \u2192 all symbols in a file                                  \u2502\n\u2502  \u251c\u2500\u2500 get_symbol_by_name  \u2192 exact node lookup                                      \u2502\n\u2502  \u251c\u2500\u2500 get_node_edges      \u2192 in/out edges for any node                             \u2502\n\u2502  \u2514\u2500\u2500 get_nodes_by_kind   \u2192 filter by type (function, interface, etc.)            \u2502\n\u2514\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2518\n```\n\n### Design Patterns\n\n- **Harness Engineering (HE):** Strict separation of visual interface and operational intelligence via custom hooks\n- **Single Responsibility Principle (SRP):** No monolithic files; every module has one job\n- **Exponential Backoff + Idempotency:** All external API calls are retry-safe\n- **Fallback-First:** If affiliate API fails \u2192 publish with organic link; never break the pipeline\n- **Hybrid Canvas State:** ReactFlow owns positions \u2192 debounced localStorage; Zustand owns run state \u2192 synced via `useEffect`\n- **Tri-Mode AI:** AIOS routes to Gemini direct \u2192 OpenRouter \u2192 edge-function proxy, in that priority order\n\n---\n\n## \ud83e\udde0 Core AIOS (Salto Qu\u00e2ntico)\n\nThe **Core** page (`/dashboard/core`) is the sovereign consciousness interface of MCORCH \u2014 a spatial computing environment with bidirectional AI conversation and reactive R3F visualization.\n\n```\n/dashboard/core  (full-canvas, sidebar collapsed)\n\u2502\n\u251c\u2500\u2500 72% \u2014 AIOSTerminal (Canvas Negro)\n\u2502     \u251c\u2500\u2500 Matrix Rain canvas \u2014 katakana + digits, requestAnimationFrame\n\u2502     \u251c\u2500\u2500 useAIOSChat streaming \u2014 tri-mode priority chain:\n\u2502     \u2502     1. VITE_GEMINI_API_KEY \u2192 Gemini 2.0 Flash REST SSE (direct)\n\u2502     \u2502     2. VITE_OPENROUTER_API_KEY \u2192 OpenRouter SSE (google/gemini-2.0-flash-001)\n\u2502     \u2502     3. Supabase JWT \u2192 openrouter-swarm edge function proxy\n\u2502     \u251c\u2500\u2500 VAD (Voice Activity Detection) \u2014 Web Speech API, pt-BR, continuous=true\n\u2502     \u251c\u2500\u2500 Barge-in \u2014 AbortController stops mid-stream on voice input\n\u2502     \u2514\u2500\u2500 Chat history \u2014 MessageBubble components with streaming cursor\n\u2502\n\u2514\u2500\u2500 28% \u2014 VibrationalWaves (React Three Fiber)\n      \u251c\u2500\u2500 CoreSphere \u2014 MeshDistortMaterial, distort \u221d conversation intensity\n      \u251c\u2500\u2500 4 FrequencyRings \u2014 torusGeometry, sin-scale + opacity pulse per harmonic\n      \u251c\u2500\u2500 ParticleField \u2014 280 pts, palette violet/cyan/fuchsia/purple\n      \u251c\u2500\u2500 WavePlane \u2014 PlaneGeometry 32\u00d732, Y = sin(dist\u00b71.2 \u2212 t\u00b72.0)\n      \u2514\u2500\u2500 Stars (drei) \u2014 800 background stars\n```\n\n### AIOS Self-Awareness\n\nAIOS understands the entire architecture: GraphRAG memory mesh, mcoCoins economy, affiliate engines, Sovereign Mesh, and the multi-agent swarm. It responds in the user's language (pt-BR or English) and can be queried about any aspect of the system in real time.\n\n### VAD + Barge-in Protocol\n\n```\nUser speaks \u2192 SpeechRecognition interim transcript \u2192 input field\nUser pauses \u2192 isFinal=true \u2192 sendMessage(transcript) auto-triggered\nUser speaks mid-response \u2192 AbortController.abort() \u2192 stream stops \u2192 new query starts\n```\n\n---\n\n## \ud83d\udcb0 Affiliate Revenue Flywheel\n\nThe complete revenue loop is now closed end-to-end:\n\n```\norchestrate-content\n  \u2514\u2500\u2500 utm_campaign={campaign_id}  \u2190 real per-campaign ID (not hardcoded)\n      utm_term={keyword}          \u2190 topic keyword injected\n      utm_source={platform}       \u2190 linkedin | twitter | wordpress\n      \u2514\u2500\u2500 process-affiliate-link\n            probeUrl (HEAD 8s) \u2192 ML product exists?\n              NO \u2192 extractKeywords \u2192 findSimilarProduct (ML Search API)\n            buildAffiliateUrl \u2192 partner_id + all UTM params\n            INSERT affiliate_links (original_url, short_url, campaign_id)\n            \u2514\u2500\u2500 content published with monetized link\n\nUser clicks link \u2192 Mercado Livre records conversion\n  \u2514\u2500\u2500 ML sends postback to:\n      https://bcyvddsykvehvpwstlfa.supabase.co/functions/v1/handle-ml-postback\n        \u251c\u2500\u2500 resolve click_id \u2192 affiliate_links.id\n        \u251c\u2500\u2500 UPDATE affiliate_links SET revenue_cents = commission\n        \u251c\u2500\u2500 UPDATE mcorch_nodes SET revenue_impact = commission\n        \u2514\u2500\u2500 INSERT knowledge mesh observation node (event: ml_conversion)\n\nROI Dashboard \u2190 affiliate_links.revenue_cents > 0 (real BRL for the first time)\n```\n\n### ML Dev Center Configuration\n\n| Field | Value |\n|-------|-------|\n| **Notification callback URL** | `https://bcyvddsykvehvpwstlfa.supabase.co/functions/v1/handle-ml-postback` |\n| **Redirect URI (OAuth)** | `https://login.mcorch.com/dashboard/affiliates` |\n\n---\n\n## \ud83e\udde0 Sovereign Mesh (Phase Gamma)\n\nThe Docker Sovereign Mesh provides the physical autonomic computing substrate:\n\n```\nDocker Sovereign Mesh (5 containers, 5+ days uptime)\n\u251c\u2500\u2500 mcorch_chroma         \u2192 Vector Engine (ChromaDB v2, port 8001)\n\u251c\u2500\u2500 mcorch_claude_mem     \u2192 Intelligence Node (ARM64, healthy)\n\u251c\u2500\u2500 mega-brain-daemon     \u2192 Autonomic Daemon (self-heal + watchdog)\n\u251c\u2500\u2500 mega-brain-webhook    \u2192 n8n webhook gateway (+10pts/run via autonomic-action)\n\u2514\u2500\u2500 mega-brain-dashboard  \u2192 n8n operational dashboard\n\nWatchdog: scripts/watchdog-mcorch.sh\n  \u2192 Monitors mcorch_claude_mem health every 5 minutes\n  \u2192 Writes telemetry to infra_health_logs (Supabase)\n  \u2192 Cron: */5 * * * * (installed on ubuntu + gcrUX)\n\nn8n autonomic-action:\n  POST /functions/v1/autonomic-action {action: \"view_content\", userId}\n  \u2192 +10pts per run \u2192 mcoCoins accumulation (feature, not bug)\n```\n\nHealth telemetry is surfaced in the **Neural HUD** (`src/components/HUD.tsx`) and read via `src/hooks/useInfraStatus.ts`.\n\n---\n\n## \ud83c\udf0c Constellation Matrix (Phase Epsilon)\n\nThe **Constellation Matrix** is a WebGL visualization of the live AST knowledge graph at `/dashboard/constellation/matrix`.\n\n```\nConstellationMatrix (Sigma.js v3 + WebGL2)\n  \u251c\u2500\u2500 526 AST nodes \u2014 file \u00b7 function \u00b7 arrow_function \u00b7 interface\n  \u251c\u2500\u2500 646 edges \u2014 DEFINES (file\u2192fn) + CALLS intra-file + CALLS cross-file\n  \u251c\u2500\u2500 ForceAtlas2 chunked layout (25 iter/tick, non-blocking)\n  \u251c\u2500\u2500 Louvain community detection (N clusters, COMMUNITY_PALETTE 12 slots)\n  \u251c\u2500\u2500 Crew Overlay \u2014 crew_agents orbiting matched AST nodes via INHABITS edges\n  \u251c\u2500\u2500 Semantic Probe \u2014 pgvector HNSW cosine search + camera fly-to on top match\n  \u251c\u2500\u2500 Ownership Territory \u2014 squad coloring (content/tech/market/ops) + pills legend\n  \u2514\u2500\u2500 CALLS counter tooltip \u2014 \u2192 N outgoing (cyan) \u00b7 \u2190 M incoming (fuchsia)\n\nRoutes:\n  /dashboard/constellation         \u2192 3D Crew Graph (React Three Fiber)\n  /dashboard/constellation/matrix  \u2192 ConstellationMatrix WebGL (Sigma.js)\n  /dashboard/constellation/memory  \u2192 Memory Mesh (d3-force SVG, full-screen)\n```\n\n### Knowledge Graph State (Delta v4)\n\n```\nmcorch_nodes: 526  (493 AST + 9 knowledge + 24 system)\nmcorch_edges: 646  (344 defines + 110 CALLS intra-file + 192 CALLS cross-file)\nNeural Density: 493/493 AST nodes embedded (100%)\n```\n\n### Delta v4 \u2014 Two-Pass Cross-File Call Resolution\n\n```\nPass 1: visitFile(file) \u2192 nodes + intra-file CALLS edges\nPost-pass: globalFunctionMap = Map<name, nodeId>\nPass 2: visitFileForCrossFileCalls \u2192 re-parses AST, walks CallExpression\n  \u2192 emits CALLS edge only if callee NOT in local map BUT IN global map\n  \u2192 dedup via edgeId hash\nResult: +259 cross-file CALLS \u00b7 646 total edges\n```\n\n---\n\n## \ud83c\udf9b Visual Orchestrator Canvas (Phase Delta)\n\nNode-based pipeline builder for campaign orchestration at `/dashboard/orchestration`.\n\n```\nOrchestrationPage (full-canvas, p-0)\n  \u2514\u2500\u2500 OrchestrationCanvas.tsx\n        \u251c\u2500\u2500 TriggerNode    \u2190 topic input + platform toggles + Run button\n        \u251c\u2500\u2500 AgentNode      \u2190 AI step display (status pulse animation, <50ms glow)\n        \u251c\u2500\u2500 SceneNode      \u2190 video scene (stability + coin cost)\n        \u251c\u2500\u2500 QualityGateNode\u2190 blocks downstream on error\n        \u251c\u2500\u2500 PublishNode    \u2190 final distribution output\n        \u2514\u2500\u2500 OrchestrationEdge \u2190 glowing bezier (violet when animated)\n\nState: ReactFlow (positions) \u2190\u2192 Zustand (run state) \u2190\u2192 orchestrate-content\nVisual latency: <50ms (status change \u2192 glow, synchronous pipeline)\n```\n\n---\n\n## \ud83d\udd2c GitNexus MCP Engine\n\nLive AST introspection of the entire codebase, available as Claude Code MCP tools.\n\n```\npackages/gitnexus-core/\n\u251c\u2500\u2500 src/\n\u2502   \u251c\u2500\u2500 mcp/server.ts           \u2190 stdio MCP server (6 tools)\n\u2502   \u251c\u2500\u2500 parser.ts               \u2190 two-pass AST ingestion\n\u2502   \u251c\u2500\u2500 languages/typescript.ts \u2190 TS/TSX visitor + extractCrossFileCalls()\n\u2502   \u251c\u2500\u2500 ast-visitor.ts          \u2190 visitFileForCrossFileCalls() wrapper\n\u2502   \u251c\u2500\u2500 adapters/supabase.ts    \u2190 syncFromGraph() \u2192 mcorch_nodes/edges\n\u2502   \u2514\u2500\u2500 cli/sync-to-mesh.ts     \u2190 npm run sync-mesh\n\nLive graph (in-memory): 3100 nodes \u00b7 2900 edges \u00b7 200 files \u00b7 1890 unique symbols\nSynced to Supabase:     526 nodes \u00b7 646 edges (project: mcorch-constellation)\n```\n\n---\n\n## \ud83d\udd78 Knowledge Mesh (GraphRAG)\n\nEvery orchestration run and every ML conversion injects a node into the relational memory graph:\n\n```\nmcorch_nodes  (user_id + project_id tenant isolation; system nodes user_id=NULL)\nmcorch_edges  (DEFINES | CALLS | implements | fixes | depends_on | mentions)\n  \u2193\nembed-mcorch-node \u2192 OpenRouter openai/text-embedding-3-small (768d)\n  \u2193\npgvector HNSW index (cosine similarity, vector(768))\n  \u2193\nstability_score + FSRS-6 decay \u00b7 revenue_impact per node\n```\n\n### Node Types\n\n| node_type | Description |\n|-----------|-------------|\n| `file` | Source file boundary (AST) |\n| `function` | Named function declaration (AST) |\n| `arrow_function` | Arrow function const fn = () => (AST) |\n| `interface` | TypeScript interface/type (AST) |\n| `milestone` | Phase boundary events (system node) |\n| `decision` | Architectural decisions |\n| `observation` | Runtime observations \u2014 orchestration runs + ML conversions |\n| `conversation` | AIOS session nodes (planned \u2014 Core v2) |\n| `feature` | Implemented features |\n| `requirement` | Planned requirements |\n\n### GraphRAG State (post-Salto Qu\u00e2ntico, 2026-05-01)\n\n- **526 AST nodes** (project: `mcorch-constellation`) \u2014 493 embedded (100%)\n- **9 knowledge nodes** (system, user_id = NULL) \u2014 all embedded\n- **646 edges** in `mcorch_edges` (344 defines + 302 CALLS)\n- **ML conversion nodes** auto-inserted by `handle-ml-postback` on every approved order\n- `revenue_impact` tracks economic weight per node\n- FSRS-6 decay active on `stability_score`\n- HNSW cosine index operational\n\n---\n\n## \ud83e\ude99 mcoCoins Economics\n\nAll paid operations are gated by an atomic RPC \u2014 no client-side deduction possible:\n\n```\nprofiles.mco_balance          \u2192 canonical billing ledger\ndeduct_mco_coins(user, amt)   \u2192 atomic RPC (SECURITY DEFINER, enforces balance check)\nsrc/lib/billing.ts \u2192 COIN_COSTS\n  orchestrate-content: 10 mcoCoins/run \u2192 HTTP 402 on insufficient balance\n```\n\n| Plan | mcoCoins |\n|------|----------|\n| Starter | 500 |\n| Pro | 2000 |\n| Enterprise | 10000 |\n\n| Operation | Cost |\n|-----------|------|\n| Orchestration run | 10 mcoCoins |\n| Content generation | 5 mcoCoins |\n| Image generation | 3 mcoCoins |\n| Node embedding | 1 mcoCoin |\n\n### Self-Service Top-Up\n\n`/dashboard/settings` \u2192 card **\"mcoCoins \u2014 Saldo Soberano\"**:\n- Saldo em tempo real (`profiles.mco_balance`)\n- Input livre + atalhos r\u00e1pidos **+500 / +2000 / +10000**\n- Update at\u00f4mico client-side (RLS garante owner-only write)\n- Hist\u00f3rico de ac\u00famulo via n8n autonomic-action (+10pts/run)\n\nThe balance is shown in real-time in the Dashboard header via Supabase Realtime subscription.\n\n---\n\n## \ud83d\udee0 Tech Stack\n\n### Frontend\n\n| Technology | Purpose |\n|------------|---------|\n| React 18 | UI framework |\n| TypeScript (Strict) | Type safety |\n| Vite 5 | Build tooling |\n| Tailwind CSS v3 | Utility-first styling |\n| shadcn/ui + Radix | Component library |\n| React Three Fiber v8 | 3D constellation graph + VibrationalWaves |\n| **Sigma.js v3 + WebGL2** | Constellation Matrix |\n| **Graphology** | In-memory graph engine (ForceAtlas2, Louvain) |\n| **@xyflow/react** | Visual Orchestrator Canvas |\n| TanStack Query | Server state management |\n| Zustand | Client state (crew + orchestration stores) |\n| Recharts | Data visualization |\n| d3-force | Memory Mesh graph layout |\n| Web Speech API | VAD + Barge-in for Core AIOS |\n\n### Backend\n\n| Technology | Purpose |\n|------------|---------|\n| Supabase (PostgreSQL + pgvector) | Database + Auth + RLS + vector search |\n| Edge Functions (Deno) \u2014 32 live | Serverless business logic |\n| Supabase Storage | Private file storage (signed URLs) |\n| Supabase Realtime | WebSocket subscriptions (mcoCoins HUD) |\n| ChromaDB (Docker) | Local vector engine |\n\n### External Integrations\n\n| Service | Purpose |\n|---------|---------|\n| Stripe | Payments & subscriptions (sandbox \u2014 live after first client) |\n| Mercado Livre | Affiliate monetization + conversion postback |\n| LinkedIn API | Social publishing |\n| Twitter/X API | Social publishing |\n| WordPress REST | Blog/article publishing |\n| OpenRouter | AI content + embeddings + AIOS fallback |\n| Gemini 2.0 Flash | AIOS primary (direct Every session starts with `/handson` and ends with `/handoff`.\n\n```\n/handson  \u2192 reads HANDOFF.md + memory index + git log + container health\n            \u2192 produces session brief in < 10 seconds\n/handoff  \u2192 granular commits by concern \u2192 updates HANDOFF.md \u2192 git push origin main\n            \u2192 bun run build for local production deploy\n```\n\n**Memory system:** `/home/ubuntu/.claude/projects/\u2026/memory/` \u2014 persistent across sessions, covering user profile, project state, feedback, and references. MEMORY.md is the index.\n\n**GitNexus MCP** is automatically available in Claude Code via `.mcp.json` (stdio, no Docker required).\n\n---\n\n## \ud83e\udd1d Contributing\n\n### Code Standards\n\n- **Language:** Code, variables, comments, logs \u2192 **English**\n- **UI:** Interface, toasts, validation \u2192 **Portuguese (Brazil)**\n- **TypeScript:** Strict; explicit types everywhere\n- **Architecture:** Harness Engineering \u2014 hooks for logic, components for display\n- **Naming:** `PascalCase` (components/interfaces) \u00b7 `camelCase` (functions/vars) \u00b7 `UPPER_SNAKE_CASE` (constants)\n\n### Commit Convention\n\n```\nfeat:          new feature\nfix:           bug fix\nfeat(edge):    edge function\nfeat(hooks):   custom hook\nfeat(ui):      component or page\nrefactor:      code restructuring\nchore:         tooling, dependencies\ndocs:          documentation (including HANDOFF.md seals)\n```\n\n---\n\n<p align=\"center\">\n  Built with Sovereignty \u00b7 Powered by Supabase + OpenRouter \u00b7 GraphRAG by MCORCH<br/>\n  GitNexus AST Intelligence \u00b7 Core AIOS Live \u00b7 Affiliate Flywheel Closed \u00b7 Phase Quantum \u2728\n</p>\n"

3. Files and Code Sections:

- **`supabase/functions/handle-ml-postback/index.ts`** (NEW — created in worktree, copied to main repo, deployed):
  - Receives ML postback (GET query params or POST JSON/form-urlencoded)
  - Resolves `click_id`/`transaction_id` to `affiliate_links.id` (UUID or ilike short_url)
  - Resolves by `order_id` as fallback
  - `revenueCents`: if raw < 100 → multiply by 100 (float BRL), else keep as-is (cents)
  - `UPDATE affiliate_links SET revenue_cents = X`
  - `UPDATE mcorch_nodes SET revenue_impact = newRevenue` (finds node by metadata.link_id or content_id)
  - `INSERT mcorch_nodes` observation node (node_type: "observation", project_id: "mcorch-affiliate")
  - Logs unresolved click_ids rather than returning 500 (prevents ML retries)
  - Returns 200 always to prevent ML from retrying

- **`supabase/functions/orchestrate-content/index.ts`** (MODIFIED):
  ```typescript
  const utmCampaign = campaign_id
    ? encodeURIComponent(campaign_id)
    : `mcorch_${topic.slice(0, 24).replace(/\s+/g, "_").toLowerCase()}`;
  const utmTag = (platform: string, baseUrl?: string) => {
    const params = `utm_source=${platform}&utm_medium=social&utm_campaign=${utmCampaign}&utm_term=${encodeURIComponent(topic.slice(0, 50))}`;
    return baseUrl ? `${baseUrl}${baseUrl.includes("?") ? "&" : "?"}${params}` : params;
  };
  ```
